# Whole-spec completion contract

Planning audit begun 2026-09-20 on shared `dev` at `be4288fb`. This covers every file recursively under `specs/`, not a release subset. The complete-product Markdown and JSON share the same normative criteria; their stale status tables are not proof. The owner-provided backend spec remains an untracked, preserved source file in this checkout; its audited hash is recorded in the source ledger.

IDs AC-001 through AC-046 are preserved. New IDs are assigned once below and must never be renumbered or deleted. A removed source criterion becomes RETIRED with a reason. No source criterion was retired in this audit. A source-key/ID/task map and source hashes live in [source-coverage.json](codex-plans/whole-spec-audit-2026-09-20/source-coverage.json).

PASS means retained, criterion-matched passing evidence with its stated scope; it does not certify broader workflows or current production. PENDING means some required implementation or proof is missing. New cases and files named below are proposed test deliverables, not tests written or run by this plan. Code state is separately recorded as missing, partial, implemented-unverified, configuration-blocked or verified in the coverage ledger. Historical PASS evidence remains dated; AC-012, AC-024, AC-029 and AC-030 reopen the specific uncovered outcomes while retaining their narrower passing evidence.

P = measurable programmatic validation, including actual persisted runtime behavior, controlled failure/race tests, browser interactions, export/render inspection and performance measurements. J = human-like judgment of clarity, tone, visual or operating quality. P+J requires both. Reuse `src/lib/llm-review.ts` with real Capsule artifacts, AUDIENCE_JTBD.md and DESIGN.md; its generic example criteria and unrun live-model sample tests are not Capsule proof. Record reviewer/model, environment/SHA, artifact, criterion, verdict and feedback. Human operator review is also valid. Never substitute export existence, source-string assertions or a generic model verdict for a real outcome.

Existing runtime-proof patterns are under `tests/proofs/`, including `proposal-event-booking.runtime.test.ts`, `safe-culinary-operations.runtime.test.ts` and `event-weekly-purchasing.runtime.test.ts`. Extend an owning behavior suite when it already covers the boundary. Future tests are authorized by this owner-requested contract; no test or application code is added by the planning audit.

Current validation (Windows and Linux CI, 2026-09-21; tested commit `fcf2238af52a457f920fc1513cc2dc3e4e6f3349` on `dev`, tag v0.0.71, draft PR #388): both build environments are green. On Windows (working tree based on `89c103bc` plus the tested increment) the full `bun run check` gate is green — typecheck, format, secrets scan, nine Manifest integration guards, coverage ratchet at 194 test files / 896 tests, frontend build, and the index-based baseline count (`baseline-decay: ok`, the #380 repair) — and `bun run manifest:regen:check` reports generated output current. On Linux CI, run [35599405184](https://github.com/Angriff36/capsule/actions/runs/35599405184) on ubuntu-latest at that exact commit succeeded with both required gates ("Verify generated output", "Run repository check gate") and the same 194/896 suite footer. Receipt: [build-gate-evidence-2026-09-21.md](codex-plans/whole-spec-audit-2026-09-20/build-gate-evidence-2026-09-21.md). This evidence satisfies exactly six criteria — AC-162, AC-356, AC-362, AC-395, AC-396 and AC-439 (the PL-BUILD rows). It is build-gate evidence only: the wider delivery is NOT complete, and every other PENDING criterion stays PENDING. Local preview is `http://127.0.0.1:7813`, served from this checkout with local Convex at `127.0.0.1:3210`.

| AC-399 | specs/capsule-backend-end-state-spec.md — BE-4.2-stages | Ten stages with one unambiguous meaning each: quote (no ordering/staffing/invoicing), planning (provisional drafts), pending_approval (no invented approvers), approved (ensure invoice draft, pack list, prep, purchasing contributions, staffing needs), sales_lock (accepted revision preserved, ops edits not frozen), executing (completion/exception/attendance actions), final (accept actuals, no new speculative plans), completed (release mutable reservations, preserve history), closed_out (only audited correction path), cancelled (stand down mutable work, preserve committed obligations); backend never conflates acceptance/approval/lock/finalization/completion/closeout | Primary proof: `tests/proofs/lifecycle-stage-meanings-matrix.runtime.test.ts` (4 tests, green 2026-09-22): quote/planning/pending_approval have 0 live invoices/packLists/purchaseNeeds/eventStaffNeeds; approved ensures 1 draft unsent invoice + 1 draft pack; sales_lock changeHeadcount→48 leaves quotedPrice 4500 / budgetAmount 3000 and changePricing is refused with zero writes; executing guest invite→rsvpConfirm→checkIn succeeds, Event_complete is refused, Event_finalizeEvent → final; final refuses EventDish_createViaAddToEvent and Event_closeOut, Event_complete → completed; closed_out refuses changePricing/changeHeadcount/cancel; cancel from approved sets pack cancelled and invoice voided (deletedAt absent-or-null). Cancel legality remains `tests/proofs/lifecycle-meanings.runtime.test.ts`. Typed transitions remain AC-400.
